Mood reactivity to daily family stressors among transgender and nonbinary parents during the COVID-19 pandemic.

Mood reactivity to daily family stressors among transgender and nonbinary parents during the COVID-19 pandemic.
作者： Shixin Fang, Tornello SL, Damon E Jones
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
The study aimed to explore how identity-based stigma related to gender affects the mood reactivity of transgender and nonbinary parents to daily family stressors during the COVID-19 pandemic. The research was motivated by the disproportionate impact of the pandemic on transgender and nonbinary individuals due to existing disparities and disruptions in gender-affirming care.
设计与对象
这项研究采用每日日记调查的方式,收集了 2020 年 5 月至 6 月期间美国 79 名跨性别和非二元父母的数据。参与者完成了基线评估和为期 8 天的跟踪调查。
核心方法
该研究采用多层次模型来分析家庭压力源与父母负面情绪在人与人之间和人与人之间的关联。它还检查了一生中遭受性别认同微侵犯是否会调节对父母间冲突的情绪反应。
主要结果
多层次建模结果表明,这两种家庭压力源都与父母在人际和人际层面上的负面情绪有关。人内关联仅是同时发生的,没有证据表明未来会产生预期影响。跨层次的互动表明,一生中性别认同的微攻击性暴露减弱了对父母间冲突的情绪反应。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,日常家庭压力过程可以作为干预目标,以支持跨性别和非二元父母家庭在公共卫生危机期间的福祉。这项研究提出了一个关于基于身份的耻辱如何与家庭压力源相互作用以影响情绪的新颖视角。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果可以为有针对性的干预措施提供信息,以在突发公共卫生事件期间支持跨性别和非二元父母的心理健康,特别是通过解决与性别认同相关的压力源对日常情绪的影响。
局限与证据强度
该研究由于依赖自我报告的数据和相对较小的样本量而受到限制。证据是中等的,因为研究结果是基于单一时间段的,可能无法推广到所有跨性别和非二元父母。

Mood reactivity to daily family stressors among transgender and nonbinary parents during the COVID-19 pandemic.

Authors: Shixin Fang, Tornello SL, Damon E Jones
Original abstract (English)
The COVID-19 pandemic disproportionately affected transgender and nonbinary (TGNB) parents due to long-standing disparities and disruptions in gender-affirming care. Heightened stress related to stigmatized gender identity during the pandemic may have generalized to additional stressors in their daily family life, such as parental stress and interparental conflict, which in turn may predict TGNB parents' daily mood. However, little is known about how identity-based stigma relates to TGNB parents' daily mood responses to family stressors. Using daily diary methods, this study examined whether parental stress and interparental conflict were associated with parental negative mood at both the between-person and within-person levels during the pandemic and whether lifetime gender identity microaggression exposure moderated mood reactivity to these family stressors. Data were drawn from 79 TGNB parents (Mage = 40.61, SD = 9.35; 85.90% White) in the United States who completed a baseline assessment and an 8-day follow-up daily diary survey from May to June 2020 during the COVID-19 pandemic. Multilevel modeling results showed that both family stressors were associated with parental negative mood at the between-person and within-person levels. Within-person associations were contemporaneous only, with no evidence of prospective next-day effects. Cross-level interactions revealed that lifetime gender identity microaggression exposure attenuated the mood reactivity to interparental conflicts, such that interparental conflict only predicted daily increases in parental negative mood for TGNB parents with lower to moderate lifetime exposure to gender identity microaggressions. These findings identify daily family stress processes as potential intervention targets to support TGNB-parent families' well-being during public health crises. (PsycInfo Database Record (c) 2026 APA, all rights reserved).
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to explore how identity-based stigma related to gender affects the mood reactivity of transgender and nonbinary parents to daily family stressors during the COVID-19 pandemic. The research was motivated by the disproportionate impact of the pandemic on transgender and nonbinary individuals due to existing disparities and disruptions in gender-affirming care.
Design and population
This study used a daily diary survey approach, collecting data from 79 transgender and nonbinary parents in the United States between May and June 2020. Participants completed a baseline assessment and an 8-day follow-up survey.
Core methods
The study employed multilevel modeling to analyze the association between family stressors and parental negative mood at both between-person and within-person levels. It also examined whether lifetime exposure to gender identity microaggressions moderated mood reactivity to interparental conflicts.
Main results
Multilevel modeling results showed that both family stressors were associated with parental negative mood at the between-person and within-person levels. Within-person associations were contemporaneous only, with no evidence of prospective next-day effects. Cross-level interactions revealed that lifetime gender identity microaggression exposure attenuated the mood reactivity to interparental conflicts.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that daily family stress processes may serve as intervention targets to support the well-being of transgender and nonbinary parent families during public health crises. This study introduces a novel perspective on how identity-based stigma interacts with family stressors to influence mood.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results can inform targeted interventions to support the mental health of transgender and nonbinary parents during public health emergencies, particularly by addressing the impact of gender identity-related stressors on daily mood.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on self-reported data and a relatively small sample size. The evidence is moderate, as the findings are based on a single time period and may not be generalizable to all transgender and nonbinary parents.

Genome-wide machine learning analysis of anosmia and ageusia with COVID-19.

Genome-wide machine learning analysis of anosmia and ageusia with COVID-19.
作者： Lucas Pietan, Elizabeth Phillippi, Manuela A. Melo, Hatem El-Shanti, Bruce A. Smith, Benjamin W. Darbro, Terry A. Braun, Thomas L. Casavant, Hatem El‐Shanti
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
The study addressed why anosmia and ageusia occur in COVID-19 and what host genetic factors underlie these sensory impairments. It aimed to fill knowledge gaps about the sensorineural mechanisms of SARS-CoV-2 infection.
设计与对象
The study analyzed human genome sequencing data from two cohorts: the University of Iowa (UI) COVID-19 cohort (N = 187) and the National Institute of Health All of Us (AoU) Research Program COVID-19 cohort (N = 947). It employed machine learning methods to classify patients based on genetic variants and genes associated with anosmia and/or ageusia.
核心方法
使用特征选择技术构建变体和基因优先级管道。使用基于排列的变量重要性(PVI)策略来评估模型,以优先考虑候选变体和基因。
主要结果
UI 队列研究 中,变异分析的接受者操作特征 (AUROC) 曲线下的最高保留测试集面积为 0.735,基因分析的最高保留测试集面积为 0.798。对于 AoU 队列研究,变异分析的 AUROC 为 0.687。
解释与创新
The analysis prioritized several novel and known candidate host genetic factors involved in immune response, neuronal signaling, and calcium signaling, supporting previously proposed hypotheses for anosmia and ageusia in COVID-19.
科研与公卫意义
The findings suggest potential genetic targets for further research into the mechanisms of sensory impairment in COVID-19, which could inform future diagnostic or therapeutic strategies.
局限与证据强度
该研究的 AUROC 值表明预测性能中等,并且证据仅限于抽象级别的数据,这限制了有关因果关系或临床适用性的结论的强度。

Genome-wide machine learning analysis of anosmia and ageusia with COVID-19.

Authors: Lucas Pietan, Elizabeth Phillippi, Manuela A. Melo, Hatem El-Shanti, Bruce A. Smith, Benjamin W. Darbro, Terry A. Braun, Thomas L. Casavant, Hatem El‐Shanti
Original abstract (English)
The COVID-19 pandemic has caused substantial worldwide disruptions in health, economy, and society, manifesting symptoms such as loss of smell (anosmia) and loss of taste (ageusia), that can result in prolonged sensory impairment. Establishing the host genetic etiology of anosmia and ageusia in COVID-19 will aid in the overall understanding of the sensorineural aspect of the disease and contribute to possible treatments or cures. By using human genome sequencing data from the University of Iowa (UI) COVID-19 cohort (N = 187) and the National Institute of Health All of Us (AoU) Research Program COVID-19 cohort (N = 947), we investigated the genetics of anosmia and/or ageusia by employing feature selection techniques to construct a novel variant and gene prioritization pipeline, utilizing machine learning methods for the classification of patients. Models were assessed using a permutation-based variable importance (PVI) strategy for final prioritization of candidate variants and genes. The highest held-out test set area under the receiver operating characteristic (AUROC) curve for models and datasets from the UI cohort was 0.735 and 0.798 for the variant and gene analysis respectively and for the AoU cohort was 0.687 for the variant analysis. Our analysis prioritized several novel and known candidate host genetic factors involved in immune response, neuronal signaling, and calcium signaling supporting previously proposed hypotheses for anosmia/ageusia in COVID-19.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addressed why anosmia and ageusia occur in COVID-19 and what host genetic factors underlie these sensory impairments. It aimed to fill knowledge gaps about the sensorineural mechanisms of SARS-CoV-2 infection.
Design and population
The study analyzed human genome sequencing data from two cohorts: the University of Iowa (UI) COVID-19 cohort (N = 187) and the National Institute of Health All of Us (AoU) Research Program COVID-19 cohort (N = 947). It employed machine learning methods to classify patients based on genetic variants and genes associated with anosmia and/or ageusia.
Core methods
Feature selection techniques were used to construct a variant and gene prioritization pipeline. Models were evaluated using a permutation-based variable importance (PVI) strategy to prioritize candidate variants and genes.
Main results
The highest held-out test set area under the receiver operating characteristic (AUROC) curve was 0.735 for the variant analysis and 0.798 for the gene analysis in the UI cohort. For the AoU cohort, the AUROC for the variant analysis was 0.687.
Interpretation and novelty
The analysis prioritized several novel and known candidate host genetic factors involved in immune response, neuronal signaling, and calcium signaling, supporting previously proposed hypotheses for anosmia and ageusia in COVID-19.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest potential genetic targets for further research into the mechanisms of sensory impairment in COVID-19, which could inform future diagnostic or therapeutic strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's AUROC values indicate moderate predictive performance, and the evidence is limited to abstract-level data, which constrains the strength of conclusions about causality or clinical applicability.

K18-hACE2 小鼠中祖先 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2、Delta 和 Omicron EG.5 变体的比较全身致病性和 宿主 趋向性

Comparative systemic pathogenicity and host tropism of ancestral SARS-CoV-2, Delta, and Omicron EG.5 variants in K18-hACE2 mice
作者： Xiaofang Peng, Qi Zhang, PingPing Zhou, Li Tang, Chumin Liang, Huifang Lin, Zhe Liu, Baisheng Li, Jiufeng Sun, Runyu Yuan
摘要中文翻译引言 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 经历持续的基因组 进化,但流行的流通变异株往往朝着致病性降低的方向进化。然而,涉及当代免疫逃逸株(如 Omicron EG.5 亚变异株,其为全球主导 XBB 谱系的后裔)与祖先株之间的直接比较 发病机制 数据仍然匮乏。方法 为阐明病毒毒力的进化轨迹,我们对三株关键病毒株进行了系统的头对头比较:祖先株(武汉谱系)、Delta 变异株以及当前流通的 Omicron EG.5。我们采用高易感 K18-hACE2 转基因小鼠模型评估疾病进展、病毒载量及组织病理学。结果 尽管在该模型中所有毒株均最终导致致死性结局,但其在疾病动力学和严重程度方面呈现出明显梯度差异。祖先株显示出最快的疾病进展、多器官(尤其是肝脏、脑、肺和肠道)中最高的病毒载量以及最严重的组织病理学损伤。Delta 变异株表现出中等表型。值得注意的是,Omicron EG.5 变异株的致病性显著减弱,其特征为显著延迟的 死亡率、较轻的临床表现以及降低的组织病毒载量,但仍保留多器官感染能力。讨论 本研究为从祖先株经由 Delta 至 Omicron EG.5 的进化轨迹中致病性显著减弱提供了直接实验证据。这些发现为新兴变异株的风险评估提供了关键见解,并强调了持续 监测 的必要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在填补关于 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 变异株比较致病性的知识空白,因基因组 进化 已导致免疫逃逸株,但其对疾病严重程度的影响尚不明确。
设计与对象
在对 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 感染高度敏感的 K18-hACE2 转基因小鼠中,对三种关键菌株(祖先野生型(武汉谱系)、Delta 和 Omicron EG.5)的体内致病性进行了头对头比较。
核心方法
该研究使用 K18-hACE2 小鼠模型来评估三种毒株的疾病进展、病毒负荷和组织病理学,从而对其致病潜力进行系统评估。
主要结果
所有菌株在小鼠模型中均进展至致死,但它们在疾病动力学和严重程度方面表现出明显的梯度,其中野生型菌株表现出最快的进展、最高的病毒载量和最严重的组织病理学损伤。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,从祖先菌株到 Delta 到 Omicron EG.5,致病性明显减弱,为这种进化趋势提供了直接的实验证据。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果为新兴变异的风险评估提供了重要的见解,并强调了正在进行的 监测 对监测致病性变化的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于动物模型,可能无法完全反映人类疾病动态,但由于对相关 宿主 系统中关键变异的直接比较,证据是强有力的。

Comparative systemic pathogenicity and host tropism of ancestral SARS-CoV-2, Delta, and Omicron EG.5 variants in K18-hACE2 mice

Authors: Xiaofang Peng, Qi Zhang, PingPing Zhou, Li Tang, Chumin Liang, Huifang Lin, Zhe Liu, Baisheng Li, Jiufeng Sun, Runyu Yuan
Original abstract (English)
Introduction SARS-CoV-2 undergoes persistent genomic evolution, yet prevalent circulating variants tend to evolve toward reduced pathogenicity. However, direct comparative pathogenesis data involving contemporary, immune-evasive strains—such as the Omicron EG.5 subvariant (a descendant of the globally dominant XBB lineage)—relative to ancestral strains remain scarce. Methods To clarify the evolutionary trajectory of viral virulence, we conducted a systematic, head-to-head comparison of the in vivo pathogenicity of three pivotal strains: the ancestral Wild-type (Wuhan lineage), Delta, and the currently circulating Omicron EG.5. We utilized the highly susceptible K18-hACE2 transgenic mouse model to assess disease progression, viral burden, and histopathology. Results Despite uniformly progressing to lethality in this model, the strains exhibited a distinct gradient in the kinetics and severity of disease. The Wild-type strain demonstrated the most rapid disease progression, highest viral loads across multiple organs (notably liver, brain, lung, and intestine), and most severe histopathological damage. The Delta variant showed an intermediate phenotype. Strikingly, the Omicron EG.5 variant displayed significantly attenuated pathogenicity, characterized by markedly delayed mortality, milder clinical presentation, and reduced tissue viral burden, yet it retained a capacity for multi-organ infection. Discussion Our study provides direct experimental evidence for a pronounced attenuation in pathogenicity along the evolutionary trajectory from Wild-type through Delta to Omicron EG.5. These findings offer crucial insights for risk assessment of emerging variants and underscore the need for ongoing surveillance.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the knowledge gap regarding the comparative pathogenicity of SARS-CoV-2 variants, as genomic evolution has led to immune-evasive strains with unclear impacts on disease severity.
Design and population
A head-to-head comparison of the in vivo pathogenicity of three pivotal strains—ancestral Wild-type (Wuhan lineage), Delta, and Omicron EG.5—was conducted in K18-hACE2 transgenic mice, which are highly susceptible to SARS-CoV-2 infection.
Core methods
The study used the K18-hACE2 mouse model to evaluate disease progression, viral burden, and histopathology across the three strains, providing a systematic assessment of their pathogenic potential.
Main results
All strains progressed to lethality in the mouse model, but they exhibited a distinct gradient in disease kinetics and severity, with the Wild-type strain showing the most rapid progression, highest viral loads, and most severe histopathological damage.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings demonstrate a clear attenuation in pathogenicity from the ancestral strain through Delta to Omicron EG.5, offering direct experimental evidence of this evolutionary trend.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results provide crucial insights for risk assessment of emerging variants and highlight the importance of ongoing surveillance to monitor changes in pathogenicity.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to an animal model, which may not fully reflect human disease dynamics, but the evidence is strong due to the direct comparison of key variants in a relevant host system.

High rates of SARS-CoV-2 reinfection in residents of long term care facilities despite robust spike-specific immunity following serial vaccination.

Authors: Gokhan Tut, Tara Lancaster, Panagiota Sylla, David Bone, Christopher Bentley, Azar Jadir, Rachel Bruton, Katie Spencer, Soumyajit Mallick, Ahmed Elzaidi
Original abstract (English)
Older adult residents of long-term care facilities (LTCFs) suffered high rates of mortality during the initial stages of the COVID-19 pandemic but their clinical risk has decreased markedly following vaccination. Here we determined humoral and cellular immunity following delivery of a 5th vaccine dose, an mRNA spike B1:BA.1 bivalent vaccine, to care home residents. The delivery of a 5th vaccine elicited a plateau of spike-specific immunity that remained broadly stable over 100 days in almost all people. Despite this, 15% of residents had a primary infection and 30% became reinfected during 6-months of follow up. These findings reveal that serial vaccine delivery can establish robust systemic spike-specific immune responses in frail older people but that this does not reliably prevent SARS-CoV-2 reinfection. As such, additional approaches should be considered to reduce reinfection risk in this vulnerable population group.

严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 心肌梗死后冠状动脉血栓的免疫血栓特征

Immunothrombotic Features of Coronary Thrombi in Myocardial Infarction after SARS-CoV-2 Vaccination
作者： Ana Blasco, Beatriz Pelacho, María-José Coronado, Ana Royuela, Paloma Martín, Andrea Matutano, Ariana Castellano, Juan M. Escudier, Carolina González-Andrés, Javier Ortega
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 疫苗接种 后 ST 段抬高型心肌梗死 (STEMI) 患者冠状动脉血栓的组织病理学和免疫血栓形成特征,因为 疫苗接种 后心肌梗死的潜在机制仍知之甚少。
设计与对象
进行了一项回顾性匹配 队列研究 研究,包括 44 名匹配的 STEMI 患者(23 名接种疫苗和 21 名未接种疫苗),这些患者在 2021 年 1 月至 2023 年 3 月期间接受了初次经皮冠状动脉介入治疗。
核心方法
使用组织病理学、免疫组织化学和用于 NET 检测的共聚焦显微镜对抽吸获得的冠状动脉血栓进行分析。在调整 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 血清学状态后评估 疫苗接种 状态与血栓特征之间的关联。
主要结果
在44名匹配的患者中,14名接种疫苗的患者(61%)和5名未接种疫苗的患者(24%;P = .01)中检测到NETs。疫苗接种与NET阳性血栓形成的几率增加相关(比值比为5.1;95%置信区间,1.36–19.45;P = .02)。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,潜在的 NET 介导的免疫血栓形成机制与 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 疫苗接种 相关,独立于经典的 疫苗 诱导的免疫血栓性血小板减少症。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果可能为未来免疫血栓机制的研究提供信息,并提高对 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 疫苗接种 与心血管并发症之间关系的理解。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到回顾性设计和潜在混杂因素的限制,但由于匹配的 队列研究 和统计调整,证据强度中等。

Immunothrombotic Features of Coronary Thrombi in Myocardial Infarction after SARS-CoV-2 Vaccination

Authors: Ana Blasco, Beatriz Pelacho, María-José Coronado, Ana Royuela, Paloma Martín, Andrea Matutano, Ariana Castellano, Juan M. Escudier, Carolina González-Andrés, Javier Ortega
Original abstract (English)
Summary Background Neutrophil extracellular traps (NETs) contribute to immunothrombosis and arterial thrombosis. Mechanisms underlying myocardial infarction after SARS-CoV-2 vaccination remain poorly understood. Objectives To investigate histopathologic and immunothrombotic features of coronary thrombi in patients with ST-elevation myocardial infarction (STEMI) after SARS-CoV-2 vaccination. Methods We performed a retrospective matched cohort study including patients with STEMI undergoing primary percutaneous coronary intervention between January 2021 and March 2023. Coronary thrombi obtained by aspiration were analyzed by histopathology, immunohistochemistry, and confocal microscopy for NET detection. Vaccinated and unvaccinated patients were matched by age and sex. Associations between vaccination status and thrombus characteristics were assessed after adjustment for SARS-CoV-2 serologic status. Results Among 44 matched patients (23 vaccinated and 21 unvaccinated), NETs were identified in 14 vaccinated patients (61%) and 5 unvaccinated patients (24%; P = .01). Vaccination was associated with increased odds of NET-positive thrombi after adjustment for SARS-CoV-2 serology (odds ratio, 5.1; 95% CI, 1.36–19.45; P = .02). No associations were observed between vaccination and polymorphonuclear cell density, fibrin deposits, plaque fragments, or anti–platelet factor 4 staining. Among patients vaccinated within 100 days before STEMI, NET-positive thrombi were associated with shorter intervals between vaccination and myocardial infarction (median [IQR], 25 [11–64] vs 57 [40–84] days; P = .02). Conclusions SARS-CoV-2 vaccination was associated with increased NET presence in coronary thrombi from patients with STEMI, suggesting a potential NET-mediated immunothrombotic mechanism independent of classical vaccine-induced immune thrombotic thrombocytopenia.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the histopathologic and immunothrombotic features of coronary thrombi in patients with ST-elevation myocardial infarction (STEMI) following SARS-CoV-2 vaccination, as mechanisms underlying myocardial infarction after vaccination remain poorly understood.
Design and population
A retrospective matched cohort study was conducted including 44 matched patients (23 vaccinated and 21 unvaccinated) with STEMI who underwent primary percutaneous coronary intervention between January 2021 and March 2023.
Core methods
Coronary thrombi obtained by aspiration were analyzed using histopathology, immunohistochemistry, and confocal microscopy for NET detection. Associations between vaccination status and thrombus characteristics were assessed after adjustment for SARS-CoV-2 serologic status.
Main results
Among 44 matched patients, NETs were identified in 14 vaccinated patients (61%) and 5 unvaccinated patients (24%; P = .01). Vaccination was associated with increased odds of NET-positive thrombi (odds ratio, 5.1; 95% CI, 1.36–19.45; P = .02).
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest a potential NET-mediated immunothrombotic mechanism associated with SARS-CoV-2 vaccination, independent of classical vaccine-induced immune thrombotic thrombocytopenia.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results may inform future studies on immunothrombotic mechanisms and improve understanding of the relationship between SARS-CoV-2 vaccination and cardiovascular complications.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and potential confounding factors, but the evidence is moderate in strength due to the matched cohort and statistical adjustments.

Impact of the COVID-19 Pandemic on Viral Respiratory Testing in Children With Abnormal Urinalysis.

Impact of the COVID-19 Pandemic on Viral Respiratory Testing in Children With Abnormal Urinalysis.
作者： Grace Truong, Sowdhamini S. Wallace, Duc T Nguyen, Kexin Guo, Michael D Dowlin, Ila Singh, Huay-ying Lo
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
The study aimed to investigate whether the use of respiratory viral panels (RVP) in children with abnormal urinalysis results increased during the COVID-19 pandemic compared to the prepandemic period, as RVPs are common, expensive tests that often do not alter treatment in such cases.
设计与对象
一项单中心回顾性队列研究研究在四级护理儿童急诊科进行,纳入2018年3月至2025年5月尿液分析结果异常的0至24个月儿童。排除患有复杂慢性病、需要重症监护或呼吸支持或脓毒症的患者。
核心方法
该研究使用中断时间序列分析来评估大流行前、大流行和大流行后时期 RVP 使用的趋势。主要结果是接受 RVP 测试的儿童比例,次要结果包括急诊室复诊、抗病毒药物 的使用以及使用医疗补助报销费用衡量的测试费用。
主要结果
在大流行前时期,接受呼吸道病毒检测(RVP)的儿童比例为25.0%(95%置信区间,23.1-27.0);在大流行期间为68.1%(95%置信区间,66.5-69.7);在大流行后时期为66.1%(95%置信区间,64.1-68.0)。与大流行前时期相比,大流行期间的比例显著上升(P < .001),但在大流行与大流行后期间无显著变化(P = .14)。仅134名患者(1.8%)接受了抗病毒药物,呼吸道病毒检测的总成本为1 037 978美元。
解释与创新
作者解释说,在大流行期间,尿液分析结果异常的儿童的 RVP 检测有所增加,并且在大流行后仍保持较高水平,这凸显了在这种情况下进行诊断管理的必要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,在大流行期间对尿液分析结果异常的儿童过度使用 RVP 检测可能会导致不必要的医疗费用,而对急诊复诊等临床结果的影响有限。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其单中心设计和回顾性而受到限制,这可能会影响普遍性。由于缺乏随机性和潜在的混杂因素,证据强度中等。

Impact of the COVID-19 Pandemic on Viral Respiratory Testing in Children With Abnormal Urinalysis.

Authors: Grace Truong, Sowdhamini S. Wallace, Duc T Nguyen, Kexin Guo, Michael D Dowlin, Ila Singh, Huay-ying Lo
Original abstract (English)
OBJECTIVE: Respiratory viral panels (RVP) are common, expensive tests that often do not change treatment, especially in children with suspected urinary tract infections. In children with abnormal urinalysis (UA) results, we hypothesize that RVP overuse existed before the COVID-19 pandemic and has risen further after. METHODS: We conducted a single-center retrospective cohort study of children aged 0 to 24 months who presented to a quaternary care children's emergency department (ED) from March 2018 to May 2025 with abnormal UA results. Children with complex chronic conditions, required intensive care or respiratory support, or sepsis were excluded. The primary outcome was the proportion of children with RVP testing within periods of prepandemic (March 2018-February 2020), pandemic (March 2020-May 2023), and postpandemic (July 2023-May 2025). Secondary outcomes included ED revisits, antiviral use, and cost of testing. We assessed trends in RVP use across the periods using interrupted time series analysis. Cost was measured using Medicaid reimbursement fees. RESULTS: Our cohort included 7496 patients. The proportion of all children who received an RVP was 25.0% (95% CI, 23.1-27.0), 68.1% (95% CI, 66.5-69.7), and 66.1% (95% CI, 64.1-68.0) during prepandemic, pandemic, and postpandemic periods, respectively. There was an increase in viral testing during the pandemic compared with prepandemic period (P < .001) but without change between pandemic and postpandemic (P = .14). Viral testing was associated with no differences in ED revisits (P = .41). Only 134 patients (1.8%) received antivirals. The total cost of respiratory viral testing was $1 037 978. CONCLUSIONS: RVP testing increased during the COVID-19 pandemic in children with abnormal UA results and remained elevated postpandemic. RVP testing was costly and associated with low antiviral use. These results highlight opportunities for diagnostic stewardship.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate whether the use of respiratory viral panels (RVP) in children with abnormal urinalysis results increased during the COVID-19 pandemic compared to the prepandemic period, as RVPs are common, expensive tests that often do not alter treatment in such cases.
Design and population
A single-center retrospective cohort study was conducted at a quaternary care children's emergency department, involving children aged 0 to 24 months with abnormal urinalysis results from March 2018 to May 2025. Patients with complex chronic conditions, requiring intensive care or respiratory support, or sepsis were excluded.
Core methods
The study used interrupted time series analysis to assess trends in RVP use across the prepandemic, pandemic, and postpandemic periods. The primary outcome was the proportion of children receiving RVP testing, while secondary outcomes included ED revisits, antiviral use, and cost of testing, measured using Medicaid reimbursement fees.
Main results
The proportion of children receiving RVP testing was 25.0% (95% CI, 23.1-27.0) during the prepandemic period, 68.1% (95% CI, 66.5-69.7) during the pandemic, and 66.1% (95% CI, 64.1-68.0) in the postpandemic period. There was a significant increase during the pandemic compared to the prepandemic period (P < .001), but no change between pandemic and postpandemic (P = .14). Only 134 patients (1.8%) received antivirals, and the total cost of respiratory viral testing was $1 037 978.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that RVP testing increased during the pandemic in children with abnormal urinalysis results and remained elevated postpandemic, highlighting the need for diagnostic stewardship in this context.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that the overuse of RVP testing in children with abnormal urinalysis results during the pandemic may have contributed to unnecessary healthcare costs, with limited impact on clinical outcomes such as ED revisits.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its single-center design and retrospective nature, which may affect generalisability. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the lack of randomisation and potential for confounding factors.

Potent type-specific de novo antibodies complement broadly reactive imprinted antibodies in immune responses to SARS-CoV-2 variants.
作者： Timothy S. Johnston, Shuk Hang Li, Mark M. Painter, Divya Swaminathan, Reilly K. Atkinson, Bernadeta Dadonaite, Shuishu Wang, Naomi R. Douek, Lucas Kampman, Robin Schlesinger
摘要中文翻译对于快速突变的病毒,如流感病毒和严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 (严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2),抗原漂移变体回忆的免疫记忆主要包括与启动株交叉反应的抗体,而不是从头引发反应,这种现象称为原始抗原罪或免疫印记。变异暴露引起的从头反应的组成和功能仍不清楚。在这里,我们在祖先印记人类中连续暴露于 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 变体后,分离并表征了数百种回忆和从头中和单克隆抗体。与祖先交叉反应(召回)抗体相比,从头变体类型特异性抗体使用更接近种系序列的不同 V(D)J 基因,有效中和未来变体并靶向不同的受体结合域表位。尽管如此,对更新的 2024-2025 年加强剂的中和反应主要是祖先交叉反应。这些结果揭示了召回和从头抗体对平衡 免疫应答 的独特贡献,并强调了更新的增强剂 疫苗 的好处,它增强了两个子集。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决有关变异暴露引起的从头反应的组成和功能的知识差距,特别是在 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 等快速突变病毒的背景下,其中免疫记忆由交叉反应抗体主导。
设计与对象
该研究在 2024 年至 2025 年间进行,对来自祖先印记人类连续暴露于 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 变体后的数百种回忆和从头中和单克隆抗体进行了分离和表征。
核心方法
研究人员使用 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 变体的连续暴露来分离和表征中和单克隆抗体,重点关注其 V(D)J 基因使用、中和效力和表位靶向。
主要结果
与祖先交叉反应抗体相比,从头变体类型特异性抗体利用更接近种系序列的 V(D)J 基因,有效中和未来变体,并靶向不同的受体结合域表位。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,从头抗体对 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 变体提供了独特的中和能力,突出了更新的加强剂 疫苗 在增强 免疫应答 方面的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果为更新增强剂 疫苗 的开发提供了信息,该增强剂可以增强召回和从头抗体子集,从而有助于更有效地抵抗新兴的 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 变体。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于抽象证据,结果基于单克隆抗体表征,可能无法完全代表天然 免疫应答 的复杂性。证据强度中等。

Potent type-specific de novo antibodies complement broadly reactive imprinted antibodies in immune responses to SARS-CoV-2 variants.

Authors: Timothy S. Johnston, Shuk Hang Li, Mark M. Painter, Divya Swaminathan, Reilly K. Atkinson, Bernadeta Dadonaite, Shuishu Wang, Naomi R. Douek, Lucas Kampman, Robin Schlesinger
Original abstract (English)
For rapidly mutating viruses such as influenza viruses and severe acute respiratory syndrome coronavirus 2 (SARS-CoV-2), immune memory recalled by antigenically drifted variants primarily comprises antibodies that cross-react to the priming strain rather than de novo elicited responses, a phenomenon termed original antigenic sin or immune imprinting. The composition and functionality of de novo responses elicited by variant exposures remain unclear. Here we isolated and characterized hundreds of recall and de novo neutralizing monoclonal antibodies after sequential exposures to SARS-CoV-2 variants in ancestral-imprinted humans. De novo variant type-specific antibodies used different V(D)J genes that were closer to germline sequence, potently neutralized future variants and targeted distinct receptor binding domain epitopes compared to ancestral cross-reactive (recall) antibodies. Nevertheless, neutralizing responses to the updated 2024-2025 booster were predominantly ancestral cross-reactive. These results reveal the distinct contributions of recall and de novo antibodies to a balanced immune response and underscore the benefit of updated booster vaccines, which augment both subsets.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the knowledge gap regarding the composition and functionality of de novo responses elicited by variant exposures, particularly in the context of rapidly mutating viruses like SARS-CoV-2 where immune memory is dominated by cross-reactive antibodies.
Design and population
The study involved isolating and characterizing hundreds of recall and de novo neutralizing monoclonal antibodies from ancestral-imprinted humans after sequential exposures to SARS-CoV-2 variants, conducted between 2024 and 2025.
Core methods
The researchers used sequential exposures to SARS-CoV-2 variants to isolate and characterize neutralizing monoclonal antibodies, focusing on their V(D)J gene usage, neutralization potency, and epitope targeting.
Main results
De novo variant type-specific antibodies utilized V(D)J genes closer to germline sequences, potently neutralized future variants, and targeted distinct receptor binding domain epitopes compared to ancestral cross-reactive antibodies.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings demonstrate that de novo antibodies provide distinct neutralizing capabilities against SARS-CoV-2 variants, highlighting the importance of updated booster vaccines in enhancing immune responses.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results inform the development of updated booster vaccines that can augment both recall and de novo antibody subsets, contributing to more effective immunity against emerging SARS-CoV-2 variants.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to abstract-only evidence, and the results are based on monoclonal antibody characterization, which may not fully represent the complexity of natural immune responses. The evidence strength is moderate.

Phase 1, first-in-human, randomized, placebo-controlled, dose-escalating study to evaluate OVX033, a nucleocapsid-based SARS-CoV-2 candidate vaccine.

Phase 1, first-in-human, randomized, placebo-controlled, dose-escalating study to evaluate OVX033, a nucleocapsid-based SARS-CoV-2 candidate vaccine.
作者： O. Launay, Emilie Piat, O. Bonduelle, Jacques Bruhwyler, Luong LB, Michèle Wokam, Jessika Tourneur, P. Moris, Béatrice Parfait, C. Paques
摘要中文翻译背景 OVX033 是一种潜在的通用 sarbecovirus 疫苗 候选病毒,针对高度保守的 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 核衣壳 (N) 蛋白。临床前研究已证明跨变异保护和良好的耐受性。这项首次人体试验评估了健康成人(18-49 岁)单次肌肉注射 OVX033 的安全性和免疫原性。方法 在这项随机、安慰剂对照、观察者盲法、序贯、剂量递增研究中,48 名之前接种过 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 许可的 疫苗 疫苗的参与者接受了 OVX033(100μg、250μg 或 500μg;每 队列研究 中 n=12)或安慰剂(每 队列研究 中 n=12)或安慰剂(n=12,每 4 例)。队列研究)。记录自发反应原性 7 天,自发不良事件 (AE) 记录 28 天,严重不良事件 (SAE) 记录 6 个月。免疫原性评估包括抗 N IgG 和 N 特异性 T 细胞反应。结果 无论剂量水平如何,OVX033 都是安全且耐受性良好的。注射部位疼痛是最常见的局部反应;疲劳、头痛和肌痛是最常见的全身症状。未发现严重的反应原性、治疗 相关的 SAE 或安全信号。OVX033 引发了强烈的记忆性抗 N IgG 反应,滴度在第 29 天达到峰值,在 67-100% 的 疫苗 中观察到滴度与基线相比上升了 4 倍。疫苗接种 后第 8 天和第 29 天测量到 N 特异性 IFNγ CD4+ T 细胞反应适度增加,而 CD8+ T 细胞反应仍低于定量极限。没有明确的 OVX033 剂量效应证据。结论 OVX033 表现出良好的安全性,并能强力增强已有的 N 特异性体液免疫。尽管实现了 N 特异性 T 细胞介导的 免疫应答,但其幅度仍然有限​​。这一限制可以通过实施两剂方案或通过 疫苗 的辅助来解决。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估 OVX033(一种基于核衣壳的 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 疫苗 候选药物)在健康成人中的安全性和免疫原性。
设计与对象
一项 1 期、首次人体、随机、安慰剂对照、剂量递增研究对 48 名参与者进行了研究,这些参与者之前接种过 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 许可的 疫苗,他们接受 OVX033(100μg、250μg 或 500μg;n=12/队列研究)或安慰剂(n=12、4/ 队列研究)。
核心方法
该研究涉及免疫原性评估,包括抗 N IgG 和 N 特异性 T 细胞反应,并监测 7 天的诱发反应原性、28 天的主动不良事件和 6 个月的严重不良事件。
主要结果
OVX033 在所有剂量水平下都是安全且耐受性良好的。注射部位疼痛是最常见的局部反应,而疲劳、头痛和肌痛是最常见的全身症状。
解释与创新
作者得出的结论是,OVX033 表现出良好的安全性,并能强烈增强已有的 N 特异性体液免疫,但没有观察到明显的剂量效应。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,OVX033 可能是增强针对 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 的现有免疫力的可行候选药物,可能有助于更广泛的 疫苗接种 策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于单剂量和小样本量,观察到的适度 T 细胞反应可能需要通过两剂量方案或辅助治疗进行进一步研究。

Phase 1, first-in-human, randomized, placebo-controlled, dose-escalating study to evaluate OVX033, a nucleocapsid-based SARS-CoV-2 candidate vaccine.

Authors: O. Launay, Emilie Piat, O. Bonduelle, Jacques Bruhwyler, Luong LB, Michèle Wokam, Jessika Tourneur, P. Moris, Béatrice Parfait, C. Paques
Original abstract (English)
Background OVX033 is a potential universal sarbecovirus vaccine candidate targeting the highly conserved SARS-CoV-2 nucleocapsid (N) protein. Preclinical studies have demonstrated cross-variant protection and favorable tolerability. This first-in-human trial evaluated the safety and immunogenicity of a single intramuscular dose of OVX033 in healthy adults (18-49 years). Methods In this randomized, placebo-controlled, observer-blind, sequential, dose-escalation study, 48 participants, previously vaccinated with SARS-CoV-2 licensed vaccines, received either OVX033 (100μg, 250μg or 500μg; n=12 per cohort) or placebo (n=12, 4 per cohort). Solicited reactogenicity was recorded for 7 days, unsolicited adverse events (AEs) for 28 days, and serious AEs (SAEs) for 6 months. Immunogenicity assessments included anti-N IgG and N-specific T-cell responses. Results OVX033 was safe and well-tolerated whatever the dose-level. Injection site pain was the most common local reaction; fatigue, headache, and myalgia were the most frequent systemic symptoms. No severe reactogenicity, treatment-related SAEs, or safety signals were identified. OVX033 elicited a robust anamnestic anti-N IgG response, with titers peaking at Day 29, and 4-fold rises versus baseline observed in 67-100% of vaccinees. Modest increases of N-specific IFNγ CD4+ T-cell responses were measured on Days 8 and 29 after vaccination, while CD8+ T-cell responses remained below the limit of quantification. No clear OVX033 dose-effect was evidenced. Conclusions OVX033 demonstrated a favorable safety profile and strong boosting of pre-existing N-specific humoral immunity. Although a N-specific T-cell-mediated immune response was achieved, it remained of limited amplitude. This limitation could be addressed by implementing a two-dose regimen, or through the adjuvantation of the vaccine.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to evaluate the safety and immunogenicity of OVX033, a nucleocapsid-based SARS-CoV-2 vaccine candidate, in healthy adults.
Design and population
A Phase 1, first-in-human, randomized, placebo-controlled, dose-escalating study was conducted with 48 participants, previously vaccinated with SARS-CoV-2 licensed vaccines, who received either OVX033 (100μg, 250μg or 500μg; n=12 per cohort) or placebo (n=12, 4 per cohort).
Core methods
The study involved immunogenicity assessments, including anti-N IgG and N-specific T-cell responses, and monitored solicited reactogenicity for 7 days, unsolicited adverse events for 28 days, and serious adverse events for 6 months.
Main results
OVX033 was safe and well-tolerated across all dose levels. Injection site pain was the most common local reaction, while fatigue, headache, and myalgia were the most frequent systemic symptoms.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors concluded that OVX033 demonstrated a favorable safety profile and strong boosting of pre-existing N-specific humoral immunity, with no clear dose-effect observed.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that OVX033 could be a viable candidate for enhancing pre-existing immunity against SARS-CoV-2, potentially contributing to broader vaccination strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was limited to a single dose and a small sample size, and the modest T-cell responses observed may require further investigation with a two-dose regimen or adjuvantation.

常规区域流行动态的系统发育地理学快速推断 基因组监测

Rapid Phylogeographic Inference of Regional Epidemic Dynamics for Routine Genomic Surveillance
作者： Max S. Y. Lau, Leke Lyu, Nicola Mueller, Zewen Liu, Lambodhar Damodaran, Wei Jin
摘要中文翻译摘要 基因组监测 已成为流行病应对的基石,但将现代测序工作产生的大型系统发育史转化为可操作的区域流行病情报仍然超出了大多数公共卫生系统的能力范围。现有的系统发育地理学方法对于日常部署的计算要求过高,在基因组数据生成和流行病学洞察之间造成了瓶颈。在这里,我们介绍 STEPHY,这是一个易于部署的图神经推理工具包,可以直接从大型病毒系统发育中快速估计区域流行病动态。通过将系统发育表示为区域级图表并整合连接区域 暴发 的信息,STEPHY 联合估计区域特定的繁殖数量、恢复率、源-库角色和最可能的索引位置——直接告知地理目标 暴发 和干预的流行病学数量。STEPHY 通过本地定制的模拟来适应不同的病原体和 监测 设置。我们通过复杂的集合种群系统的模拟研究来验证 STEPHY,证明了区域流行病学数量的准确恢复,并表明联合建模跨区域依赖关系大大优于独立的逐区域分析。我们将 STEPHY 应用到丹麦的五个密集采样的 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 谱系系统发育中,这是有记录以来测序最全面的国家流行病之一,在明显不同的流行病条件下跨越了 Alpha、Delta 和 Omicron 波。STEPHY 一致认为 Hovedstaden 是病毒传播的主要来源,其来源作用从 Alpha 到 Omicron 变得越来越明显,揭示了连续变异波中空间 传播 异质性的逐渐增强。包含 571 至 7,177 个序列的系统发育推断仅需几毫秒,证实了 STEPHY 可满足现实世界 基因组监测 的需求。总之,这些结果使 STEPHY 成为一个可扩展且易于部署的工具包,用于使用常规 基因组监测 数据进行区域流行病推断和响应。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
基因组监测 已成为流行病应对的基石,但将大型系统发育史转化为可操作的区域流行病情报仍然超出了大多数公共卫生系统的能力范围。
设计与对象
现有的系统发育地理学方法对于日常部署的计算要求过高,在基因组数据生成和流行病学洞察之间造成了瓶颈。
核心方法
在这里,我们介绍 STEPHY,这是一个易于部署的图神经推理工具包,可以直接从大型病毒系统发育中快速估计区域流行病动态。
主要结果
我们通过复杂的集合种群系统的模拟研究来验证 STEPHY,证明了区域流行病学数量的准确恢复,并表明联合建模跨区域依赖关系大大优于独立的逐区域分析。
解释与创新
通过将系统发育表示为区域级图表并整合连接区域 暴发 的信息,STEPHY 联合估计区域特定的繁殖数量、恢复率、源-库角色和最可能的索引位置——直接告知地理目标 暴发 和干预的流行病学数量。
科研与公卫意义
包含 571 至 7,177 个序列的系统发育推断仅需几毫秒,证实了 STEPHY 可满足现实世界 基因组监测 的需求。
局限与证据强度
STEPHY 一致认为 Hovedstaden 是病毒传播的主要来源,其来源作用从 Alpha 到 Omicron 变得越来越明显,揭示了连续变异波中空间 传播 异质性的逐渐增强。

Rapid Phylogeographic Inference of Regional Epidemic Dynamics for Routine Genomic Surveillance

Authors: Max S. Y. Lau, Leke Lyu, Nicola Mueller, Zewen Liu, Lambodhar Damodaran, Wei Jin
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Genomic surveillance has become a cornerstone of epidemic response, yet translating the large phylogenies generated by modern sequencing efforts into actionable regional epidemic intelligence remains beyond the reach of most public health systems. Existing phylogeographic methods are too computationally demanding for routine deployment, creating a bottleneck between genomic data generation and epidemiological insight. Here, we introduce STEPHY, a readily deployable graph neural inference toolkit that enables rapid estimation of regional epidemic dynamics directly from large viral phylogenies. By representing phylogenies as region-level graphs and integrating information across connected regional outbreaks, STEPHY jointly estimates region-specific reproduction numbers, recovery rates, source-sink roles, and the most likely index location — epidemiological quantities that directly inform geographically targeted surveillance and intervention. STEPHY is adaptable to diverse pathogens and surveillance settings through locally tailored simulations. We validate STEPHY through simulation studies across complex metapopulation systems, demonstrating accurate recovery of the regional epidemiological quantities and showing that jointly modeling cross-regional dependencies substantially outperforms independent region-by-region analysis. We apply STEPHY to five densely sampled SARS-CoV-2 lineage phylogenies from Denmark, one of the most comprehensively sequenced national epidemics on record, spanning the Alpha, Delta, and Omicron waves under markedly different epidemic conditions. STEPHY consistently identifies Hovedstaden as the dominant source of viral dissemination, with its source role becoming increasingly pronounced from Alpha through Omicron, revealing a progressive strengthening of spatial transmission heterogeneity across successive variant waves. Inference across phylogenies containing 571 to 7,177 sequences requires only milliseconds, confirming that STEPHY scales to the demands of real-world genomic surveillance. Together, these results establish STEPHY as a scalable and readily deployable toolkit for regional epidemic inference and response using routine genomic surveillance data.

Interpretation and novelty

Limitations and evidence strength

Outcomes of Liver Cirrhosis Patients Hospitalized for Influenza Compared With COVID-19 Between 2020 and 2021 in the United States.

Outcomes of Liver Cirrhosis Patients Hospitalized for Influenza Compared With COVID-19 Between 2020 and 2021 in the United States.
作者： Renuka Verma, Kamleshun Ramphul, Kyaw M Tun, Yasmeen Obeidat, Lily Liu, Hemamalini Sakthivel, Tejas Joshi
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
Patients with liver cirrhosis have been reported to experience severe outcomes from both COVID-19 and influenza, but comparative risks between these infections in this population remain insufficiently characterized in the United States.
设计与对象
This retrospective comparative study used the National Inpatient Sample (NIS) from 2016 to 2021 to identify cirrhosis patients hospitalized for influenza (2016–2019) or COVID-19 (2020–2021) in the United States.
核心方法
Baseline characteristics and outcomes were compared between propensity-score matched cohorts of cirrhosis patients with COVID-19 and influenza using the NIS dataset.
主要结果
Among 59,590 cirrhosis patients, 6,800 (11.4%) had influenza and 52,790 (88.6%) had COVID-19; after matching, 6,670 cases were included in each group, with COVID-19 patients showing higher risks of hyponatremia, acute myocardial infarction, cardiogenic shock, severe septic shock, mechanical ventilation, cardiac arrest, acute ischemic stroke, acute kidney injury, and all-cause death, but fewer cases of ascites and hepatorenal syndrome.
解释与创新
The study demonstrates that cirrhosis patients hospitalized with COVID-19 exhibit distinct complication profiles and higher mortality risk compared to those hospitalized with influenza, highlighting a need for targeted management strategies.
科研与公卫意义
These findings suggest that cirrhosis patients may require enhanced monitoring and preventive measures during COVID-19 surges, and that comparative risk assessment between respiratory viruses in this vulnerable group is essential for clinical decision-making.
局限与证据强度
对行政索赔数据的依赖可能会引入编码错误和无法测量的混杂,并且回顾性设计限制了因果推断;因此,证据强度中等。

Outcomes of Liver Cirrhosis Patients Hospitalized for Influenza Compared With COVID-19 Between 2020 and 2021 in the United States.

Authors: Renuka Verma, Kamleshun Ramphul, Kyaw M Tun, Yasmeen Obeidat, Lily Liu, Hemamalini Sakthivel, Tejas Joshi
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Early studies during the COVID-19 pandemic showed that liver cirrhosis patients were at risk of more severe outcomes of the disease. Past studies have also linked cirrhosis patients with poorer influenza outcomes following admissions. AIM: To compare how patients with liver cirrhosis fared when admitted for COVID-19 as compared with influenza in the United States. METHODS: The National Inpatient Sample (NIS) from 2016 to 2021 was used. The influenza group of cirrhosis patients was extracted for admissions between 2016 and 2019, while the COVID-19 group involved hospitalizations in 2020 and 2021. Baseline characteristics and differences in outcomes were compared between cirrhosis patients with COVID-19 versus influenza. RESULTS: We recruited 59,590 cirrhosis patients, among whom 6800 (11.4%) had influenza and 52790 (88.6%) were infected with COVID-19. COVID-19 cases were younger, with more patients having dyslipidemia, diabetes, chronic kidney disease (CKD), obesity, and dementia, but fewer patients were smokers, peripheral vascular disease (PVD), history of stroke, history of myocardial infarction, drug abuse, alcohol abuse, chronic ischemic heart disease (IHD), asthma, COPD, and cancer. Our propensity-score matched sample had 6670 cases with COVID-19 and 6670 with influenza. COVID-19 patients were more likely to suffer from hyponatremia, acute myocardial infarction, cardiogenic shock, severe septic shock, mechanical ventilation use, prolonged mechanical ventilation use, cardiac arrest, acute ischemic stroke, acute kidney injury, and all-cause death, but fewer cases with ascites, and hepatorenal syndrome. CONCLUSIONS: The characteristics of cirrhosis patients admitted with COVID-19 differed from those admitted for influenza, with COVID-19 showing a higher mortality risk and varying differences in other complications.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Patients with liver cirrhosis have been reported to experience severe outcomes from both COVID-19 and influenza, but comparative risks between these infections in this population remain insufficiently characterized in the United States.
Design and population
This retrospective comparative study used the National Inpatient Sample (NIS) from 2016 to 2021 to identify cirrhosis patients hospitalized for influenza (2016–2019) or COVID-19 (2020–2021) in the United States.
Core methods
Baseline characteristics and outcomes were compared between propensity-score matched cohorts of cirrhosis patients with COVID-19 and influenza using the NIS dataset.
Main results
Among 59,590 cirrhosis patients, 6,800 (11.4%) had influenza and 52,790 (88.6%) had COVID-19; after matching, 6,670 cases were included in each group, with COVID-19 patients showing higher risks of hyponatremia, acute myocardial infarction, cardiogenic shock, severe septic shock, mechanical ventilation, cardiac arrest, acute ischemic stroke, acute kidney injury, and all-cause death, but fewer cases of ascites and hepatorenal syndrome.
Interpretation and novelty
The study demonstrates that cirrhosis patients hospitalized with COVID-19 exhibit distinct complication profiles and higher mortality risk compared to those hospitalized with influenza, highlighting a need for targeted management strategies.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings suggest that cirrhosis patients may require enhanced monitoring and preventive measures during COVID-19 surges, and that comparative risk assessment between respiratory viruses in this vulnerable group is essential for clinical decision-making.
Limitations and evidence strength
The reliance on administrative claims data may introduce coding errors and unmeasured confounding, and the retrospective design limits causal inference; the evidence strength is therefore moderate.

Association between post-vaccination adverse events and subsequent COVID-19 booster uptake in the Netherlands.

Association between post-vaccination adverse events and subsequent COVID-19 booster uptake in the Netherlands.
作者： Anne J Huiberts, Maud M J Smits, Christina E Hoeve, Hester E de Melker, Susan van den Hof, Mirjam J Knol
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
This study aimed to assess whether the occurrence, severity, and duration of AE following COVID-19 booster vaccination were associated with subsequent booster uptake during the 2022-2024 autumn booster campaigns in the Netherlands.
设计与对象
We included participants from the Vaccine Study COVID-19 (VASCO), an ongoing prospective cohort study, who were eligible for booster vaccination in 2022-2024. Participants completed a questionnaire on AE one month after booster vaccination.
核心方法
进行逻辑回归以评估局部和全身 AE 的发生率、严重程度和持续时间与随后一年的重新接种之间的关联。我们根据人口统计数据和之前的 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 感染情况进行了调整。
主要结果
2022 年和 2023 年,共有 11,010 名和 14,824 名参与者完成疫苗接种并填写不良事件(AE)问卷。其中,73.7% 和 87.0% 的参与者在次年进行了再次疫苗接种。2022 年全身性不良事件的发生与 2023 年再次疫苗接种的较低可能性相关(优势比:0.84 [95% 置信区间,0.76-0.93])。再次疫苗接种的可能性随不良事件严重程度增加而下降——中度不良事件(0.79 [0.66-0.94])和重度不良事件(0.45 [0.34-0.60])——以及全身性不良事件持续时间延长(>5 天:0.72 [0.54-0.95])。此外,重度局部不良事件(0.38 [0.23-0.60])及因不良事件就医(0.50 [0.34-0.73])也与 疫苗接种 接种率下降相关。类似关联在 2024 年再次疫苗接种中亦有观察。
解释与创新
This study found a lower likelihood of subsequent COVID-19 booster uptake after experiencing systemic AE, particularly when severe or prolonged, or when experiencing a severe local AE.
科研与公卫意义
这些发现强调了在沟通策略中解决经验丰富的 AE 的必要性,因为这可能会影响 疫苗接种 活动的成功。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告局限性。

Association between post-vaccination adverse events and subsequent COVID-19 booster uptake in the Netherlands.

Authors: Anne J Huiberts, Maud M J Smits, Christina E Hoeve, Hester E de Melker, Susan van den Hof, Mirjam J Knol
Original abstract (English)
INTRODUCTION: This study aimed to assess whether the occurrence, severity, and duration of AE following COVID-19 booster vaccination were associated with subsequent booster uptake during the 2022-2024 autumn booster campaigns in the Netherlands. METHODS: We included participants from the Vaccine Study COVID-19 (VASCO), an ongoing prospective cohort study, who were eligible for booster vaccination in 2022-2024. Participants completed a questionnaire on AE one month after booster vaccination. Logistic regression was performed to assess the association between the occurrence, severity, and duration of local and systemic AE, and revaccination during the subsequent year. We adjusted for demographics and prior SARS-CoV-2 infection. RESULTS: In 2022 and 2023, 11,010 and 14,824 participants were vaccinated and completed the AE questionnaire. Of these, 73.7% and 87.0% were revaccinated in subsequent years. The occurrence of systemic AE in 2022 was associated with a lower odds of revaccination in 2023 (OR: 0.84 [95%CI, 0.76-0.93]). The likelihood of revaccination declined with increasing severity- moderate (0.79 [0.66-0.94]) and severe AE (0.45 [0.34-0.60]) - and longer duration (>5 days: 0.72 [0.54-0.95]) of systemic AE. Also, severe local AE (0.38 [0.23-0.60]) and seeking medical care for AE (0.50 [0.34-0.73]) were associated with reduced vaccination uptake. Similar associations were observed for revaccination in 2024. CONCLUSION: This study found a lower likelihood of subsequent COVID-19 booster uptake after experiencing systemic AE, particularly when severe or prolonged, or when experiencing a severe local AE. These findings underscore the need to address experienced AE in communication strategies, as this may influence the success of vaccination campaigns.
View research seven-element analysis

This study aimed to assess whether the occurrence, severity, and duration of AE following COVID-19 booster vaccination were associated with subsequent booster uptake during the 2022-2024 autumn booster campaigns in the Netherlands.

Main results

The occurrence of systemic AE in 2022 was associated with a lower odds of revaccination in 2023 (OR: 0.84 [95%CI, 0.76-0.93]). The likelihood of revaccination declined with increasing severity - moderate (0.79 [0.66-0.94]) and severe AE (0.45 [0.34-0.60]) - and longer duration (>5 days: 0.72 [0.54-0.95]) of systemic AE. Also, severe local AE (0.38 [0.23-0.60]) and seeking medical care for AE (0.50 [0.34-0.73]) were associated with reduced vaccination uptake. Similar associations were observed for revaccination in 2024.

Scientific and public-health significance
These findings underscore the need to address experienced AE in communication strategies, as this may influence the success of vaccination campaigns.

The supplied evidence does not report limitations.

Emergence of COVID-19 variant XFG not linked to higher rates of sore throat or altered severity: Evidence from participatory surveillance in the Netherlands.

Emergence of COVID-19 variant XFG not linked to higher rates of sore throat or altered severity: Evidence from participatory surveillance in the Netherlands.
作者： Gesa Carstens, Eva Kozanli, Wanda G. H. Han, Marie Gruters, van Gageldonk R, Susan van den Hof, Albert Jan van Hoek, Dirk Eggink, Rianne van Gageldonk-Lafeber
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在调查 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 变体 XFG 的出现是否与喉咙痛发生率较高或疾病严重程度改变有关,如在线新闻报道和社交媒体所表明的那样。
设计与对象
参与性 监测 计划 Infectieradar 在 2024 年和 2025 年的两个特定时期收集了荷兰 39,937 名参与者的呼吸道症状和疾病严重程度的每周报告。
核心方法
参与者报告了 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 自检结果,并对有症状的代表性子样本进行了分子诊断测试和测序。使用调整后的比值比和估计的边际平均值比较两个时期之间的症状 患病率、负担和严重程度。
主要结果
与 2024 年相比,2025 年浪潮中喉咙痛的情况并没有明显增加,其他症状也没有显示出显着差异。两个时期的症状数量和疾病严重程度相当。
解释与创新
作者根据症状和严重程度的比较得出结论,喉咙痛并不代表 XFG 变异,并且 2025 年浪潮期间的疾病严重程度并不比 2024 年更严重。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,XFG 变异不会导致喉咙痛或疾病严重程度显着增加,这可能会为公众健康 暴发 和未来 暴发 的临床预期提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于自我报告的数据,不包括所有病例的实验室确认,这可能会引入偏差。由于依赖观察数据并且可能存在未测量的混杂因素,证据是中等的。

Emergence of COVID-19 variant XFG not linked to higher rates of sore throat or altered severity: Evidence from participatory surveillance in the Netherlands.

Authors: Gesa Carstens, Eva Kozanli, Wanda G. H. Han, Marie Gruters, van Gageldonk R, Susan van den Hof, Albert Jan van Hoek, Dirk Eggink, Rianne van Gageldonk-Lafeber
Original abstract (English)
Here, we investigate symptoms linked to the new SARS-CoV-2 variant XFG using an evidence-based method to determine possible elevated levels of sore throat and severity as suggested by on-line news reports and social media. Participants (n = 39,937) in the participatory surveillance program, Infectieradar, weekly report respiratory symptoms and severity of disease. In addition, SARS-CoV-2 self-test results are reported and a representative subsample of all participants with symptoms sent a nose-throat swab for molecular diagnostic testing and sequencing. We compared symptom prevalence, symptom burden and severity of disease in week 28 to week 40 in 2025, when SARS-CoV-2 strain XFG was dominant, with the same period in 2024, in which the KP.3 variant was most abundant. We compared these two periods through adjusted odds ratios and estimated marginal means to provide evidence for possibly altered symptomatology or disease severity. Sore throat was not significantly higher during the COVID-19 wave in 2025, compared to 2024, nor were any other symptoms. Additionally, the number of symptoms and severity of disease in the two periods is comparable. We conclude that sore throat is not indicative for the emerging XFG variant, nor that worse severity of disease is experienced during current COVID-19 cases in the general population.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to investigate whether the emergence of the SARS-CoV-2 variant XFG was associated with higher rates of sore throat or altered disease severity, as suggested by online news reports and social media.
Design and population
A participatory surveillance program, Infectieradar, collected weekly reports of respiratory symptoms and disease severity from 39,937 participants in the Netherlands during two specific periods in 2024 and 2025.
Core methods
Participants reported SARS-CoV-2 self-test results and a representative subsample with symptoms underwent molecular diagnostic testing and sequencing. Symptom prevalence, burden, and severity were compared between the two periods using adjusted odds ratios and estimated marginal means.
Main results
Sore throat was not significantly higher during the 2025 wave compared to 2024, and no other symptoms showed significant differences. The number of symptoms and disease severity in the two periods were comparable.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors concluded that sore throat is not indicative of the XFG variant and that disease severity during the 2025 wave was not worse than in 2024, based on the comparison of symptomatology and severity.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that the XFG variant does not cause a notable increase in sore throat or disease severity, which may inform public health surveillance and clinical expectations during future outbreaks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on self-reported data and does not include laboratory confirmation for all cases, which may introduce bias. The evidence is moderate due to the reliance on observational data and the potential for unmeasured confounding factors.

Effect of Regional COVID-19 Lockdowns on Alcohol Use and Smoking in Two Chilean University Communities: A Difference-In-Difference Analysis.

Effect of Regional COVID-19 Lockdowns on Alcohol Use and Smoking in Two Chilean University Communities: A Difference-In-Difference Analysis.
作者： Sebastián Peña, Daniela Palet, Camila Salazar-Fernández, Francisca Román Mella
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
Effects of lockdowns on alcohol use and smoking remain unclear. By exploiting the variation in regional COVID-19 lockdowns in two Chilean regions, we examined their effect on alcohol use and smoking in two university communities.
设计与对象
我们在智利两个地区科金博和阿劳卡尼亚的大学社区进行了一项准实验研究。参与者从 2020 年 7 月 (t1) 到 2021 年 4 月 (t7) 进行了七次纵向 队列研究 (n = 896) 评估。
核心方法
结果是每周的饮酒量、间歇性饮酒量 (HED) 和每天吸烟的量。
主要结果
与没有封锁的情况相比,t1 和 t3 之间的封锁与每周饮酒量增加 15.6 克(95% 置信区间 1.8;29.3)以及 t3(2020 年 9 月)每天吸烟量增加 0.3 支(95% 置信区间 0.003;0.5)相关。当封锁在 t5 逆转时,我们发现微弱的证据表明封锁区域内饮酒和吸烟有所减少。在研究期间我们没有观察到 HED 的变化。
解释与创新
COVID-19 lockdown appears to result in an initial relative increase in weekly volume of alcohol use and cigarette smoking compared to a region affected by the COVID-19 pandemic but without lockdown. We did not find evidence of changes in HED.
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明,封锁可能会影响大学社区的饮酒和吸烟行为,这可以为减轻大流行期间有害行为的公共卫生策略提供信息。结果可能有助于了解封锁对行为的影响并指导有针对性的干预措施。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告研究设计的局限性或证据的强度。该研究基于纵向 队列研究 并使用双重差分分析,该分析在控制混杂变量方面可能存在局限性。

Effect of Regional COVID-19 Lockdowns on Alcohol Use and Smoking in Two Chilean University Communities: A Difference-In-Difference Analysis.

Authors: Sebastián Peña, Daniela Palet, Camila Salazar-Fernández, Francisca Román Mella
Original abstract (English)
INTRODUCTION: Effects of lockdowns on alcohol use and smoking remain unclear. By exploiting the variation in regional COVID-19 lockdowns in two Chilean regions, we examined their effect on alcohol use and smoking in two university communities. METHODS: We conducted a quasi-experimental study in university communities in two Chilean regions, Coquimbo and Araucanía. Participants were a longitudinal cohort (n = 896) assessed seven times from July 2020 ( t 1 ) to April 2021 ( t 7 ). Outcomes were weekly grams of alcohol use, heavy episodic drinking (HED) and cigarettes smoked per day. RESULTS: Lockdown between t 1 and t 3 was associated with an increase of 15.6 g of alcohol used per week (95% confidence interval 1.8; 29.3) and an increase of 0.3 daily cigarettes smoked (95% confidence interval 0.003; 0.5) at t 3 (September 2020), compared to the absence of lockdown. When lockdown reversed at t 5 , we found weak evidence of a reduction in alcohol use and smoking in the region under lockdown. We did not observe changes in HED during the study period. DISCUSSION AND CONCLUSIONS: COVID-19 lockdown appears to result in an initial relative increase in weekly volume of alcohol use and cigarette smoking compared to a region affected by the COVID-19 pandemic but without lockdown. We did not find evidence of changes in HED. TRIAL REGISTRATION: ClinicalTrials.gov, identifier NCT05069090.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Effects of lockdowns on alcohol use and smoking remain unclear. By exploiting the variation in regional COVID-19 lockdowns in two Chilean regions, we examined their effect on alcohol use and smoking in two university communities.
Design and population
We conducted a quasi-experimental study in university communities in two Chilean regions, Coquimbo and Araucanía. Participants were a longitudinal cohort (n = 896) assessed seven times from July 2020 (t1) to April 2021 (t7).
Core methods
Outcomes were weekly grams of alcohol use, heavy episodic drinking (HED) and cigarettes smoked per day.
Main results
Lockdown between t1 and t3 was associated with an increase of 15.6 g of alcohol used per week (95% confidence interval 1.8; 29.3) and an increase of 0.3 daily cigarettes smoked (95% confidence interval 0.003; 0.5) at t3 (September 2020), compared to the absence of lockdown. When lockdown reversed at t5, we found weak evidence of a reduction in alcohol use and smoking in the region under lockdown. We did not observe changes in HED during the study period.
Interpretation and novelty
COVID-19 lockdown appears to result in an initial relative increase in weekly volume of alcohol use and cigarette smoking compared to a region affected by the COVID-19 pandemic but without lockdown. We did not find evidence of changes in HED.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings suggest that lockdowns may influence alcohol use and smoking behaviors in university communities, which could inform public health strategies for mitigating harmful behaviors during pandemics. The results may help in understanding the behavioral impacts of lockdowns and guide targeted interventions.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report on the limitations of the study design or the strength of the evidence. The study is based on a longitudinal cohort and uses a difference-in-difference analysis, which may have limitations in controlling for confounding variables.

一项回顾性队列研究研究:孕期感染严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2孕妇的新生儿调查

A Retrospective Cohort Study: Neonatal survey of pregnant women infected with SARS-CoV-2 during pregnancy
作者： Yanchao Mu, Baofeng Wang, Ruifeng Chen, Na Li, Huiping An, Guanghui Zhou, Ming Guo, Xue Li
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒 2 (严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2) 感染会增加妊娠期不良结局的风险。然而,关于孕产妇感染后新生儿健康的综合数据仍然有限。目的 比较 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 感染和未感染孕妇的孕产妇并发症和新生儿结局。方法 我们对实验室确诊的 队列研究 感染和未感染的孕妇进行了回顾性 队列研究 研究。对产妇并发症和新生儿身体指标/疾病进行了系统分析(显着性:p 结果 产妇结局:与未感染对照相比,严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 感染的妇女的并发症发生率显着升高(p < 0.05)。感染母亲的足月和早产儿的 发病率 升高:心肌酶谱异常、新生儿肺炎、中心房间隔缺损、新生儿高胆红素血症、新生儿凝血结论 母亲 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 感染显着增加早产风险和新生儿发病率。妊娠早期接触可能会导致妊娠晚期并发症。这些发现强调需要加强产前 监测 和长期后代随访。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了公众健康问题,即怀孕期间 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 感染可能会增加孕产妇和新生儿不良结局的风险,并指出孕产妇感染后全面的新生儿健康数据仍然有限。
设计与对象
一项回顾性队列研究研究比较了实验室确诊感染严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2的孕妇与未感染对照组,系统分析了孕妇并发症与新生儿结局。
核心方法
作者回顾性识别了 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 感染和未感染的孕妇,并使用标准临床评估系统分析了孕产妇并发症和新生儿身体指标和疾病。
主要结果
感染严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2的妇女的并发症发生率显着高于未感染对照,其足月和早产儿的心肌酶模式异常、新生儿肺炎、中心房间隔缺损、新生儿高胆红素血症、新生儿凝血疾病和新生儿呼吸衰竭的发病率升高。
解释与创新
作者得出的结论是,孕产妇 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 感染显着增加早产风险和新生儿发病率,孕早期接触可能会导致妊娠晚期并发症。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现支持加强产前 监测 和长期后代随访,以减轻与孕产妇 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 感染相关的风险。
局限与证据强度
回顾性设计和对仅抽象证据的依赖限制了因果推断和普遍性,保证了对所报告的关联的适度信心。

A Retrospective Cohort Study: Neonatal survey of pregnant women infected with SARS-CoV-2 during pregnancy

Authors: Yanchao Mu, Baofeng Wang, Ruifeng Chen, Na Li, Huiping An, Guanghui Zhou, Ming Guo, Xue Li
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Severe acute respiratory syndrome coronavirus 2 (SARS-CoV-2) infection increases risks of adverse outcomes in pregnancy. However, comprehensive data on neonatal health following maternal infection remain limited. Objectives To compare maternal complications and neonatal outcomes between SARS-CoV-2-infected and uninfected pregnant women. Methods We conducted a retrospective cohort study of pregnant women with and without laboratory-confirmed SARS-CoV-2 infection. Maternal complications and neonatal physical indicators/diseases were systematically analyzed (significance: p Results Maternal outcomes: SARS-CoV-2-infected women had significantly higher complication rates vs. uninfected controls (p 0.05). Term and preterm infants of infected mothers showed elevated incidences of: myocardial enzyme pattern abnormalities, neonatal pneumonia, central atrial septal defect, neonatal hyperbilirubinemia, neonatal clotting diseases, neonatal respiratory failure (p Conclusions Maternal SARS-CoV-2 infection significantly increases preterm birth risk and neonatal morbidity. First-trimester exposure may drive late-pregnancy complications. These findings underscore the need for enhanced prenatal surveillance and long-term offspring follow-up.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addressed the public-health concern that SARS-CoV-2 infection during pregnancy may elevate risks for adverse maternal and neonatal outcomes, noting that comprehensive neonatal health data following maternal infection remain limited.
Design and population
A retrospective cohort study compared pregnant women with laboratory-confirmed SARS-CoV-2 infection to uninfected controls, systematically analyzing maternal complications and neonatal outcomes.
Core methods
The authors retrospectively identified SARS-CoV-2-infected and uninfected pregnant women and systematically analyzed maternal complications and neonatal physical indicators and diseases using standard clinical assessments.
Main results
SARS-CoV-2-infected women had significantly higher complication rates than uninfected controls, and their term and preterm infants showed elevated incidences of myocardial enzyme pattern abnormalities, neonatal pneumonia, central atrial septal defect, neonatal hyperbilirubinemia, neonatal clotting diseases, and neonatal respiratory failure.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors concluded that maternal SARS-CoV-2 infection significantly increases preterm birth risk and neonatal morbidity, with first-trimester exposure potentially driving late-pregnancy complications.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings support enhanced prenatal surveillance and long-term offspring follow-up to mitigate risks associated with maternal SARS-CoV-2 infection.
Limitations and evidence strength
The retrospective design and reliance on abstract-only evidence limit causal inference and generalizability, warranting moderate confidence in the reported associations.

2024-2025 BNT162b2 KP.2 COVID-19 full season vaccine effectiveness from vaccine registries linked to administrative claims in two states: A cohort study in non-immunocompromised adults.

2024-2025 BNT162b2 KP.2 COVID-19 full season vaccine effectiveness from vaccine registries linked to administrative claims in two states: A cohort study in non-immunocompromised adults.
作者： Tara Ahi, Andersen KM, Mateus JS, Tiange Yu, Anan Zhou, Nepal RM, Santiago M. C. Lopez, L. Puzniak
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
The study aimed to assess the effectiveness of the BNT162b2 KP.2 vaccine against COVID-19-associated hospital admissions, emergency department visits, and urgent care encounters during the 2024-2025 respiratory season, as data on this topic are limited, especially among individuals with underlying medical conditions.
设计与对象
一项回顾性 队列研究 研究在路易斯安那州和加利福尼亚州的非免疫功能低下成年人中进行,自 2024 年 8 月 22 日起,在 HealthVerity 索赔数据库中连续投保≥1 年。队列研究 包括 6,256,421 人,其中 330,565 人 (5%) 接受了 BNT162b2 疫苗。
核心方法
该研究使用时变暴露方法,使用 ICD-10-CM 代码 U07.1 计算 疫苗 对入院、急诊室或紧急护理遭遇的有效性。调整后的风险比是通过 Cox 比例风险模型估计的,考虑了年龄组、性别、州、保险支付人、是否存在潜在的医疗状况以及之前的医疗保健利用情况。
主要结果
BNT162b2 对抗住院治疗的有效性为 37%,对抗急诊室/紧急护理的有效性为 12%,对抗所有三种结局的有效性为 16%。这些结果在不同年龄组和基础健康状况下是一致的。
解释与创新
The authors concluded that the BNT162b2 vaccine provided protection against COVID-19-associated outcomes, including hospitalization, emergency department, and urgent care visits, among non-immunocompromised U.S. adults during the 2024-2025 respiratory virus season.
科研与公卫意义
The findings support continued vaccine recommendations for non-immunocompromised adults, including those with underlying medical conditions, during the 2024-2025 respiratory season, highlighting the vaccine's role in reducing severe outcomes of COVID-19.
局限与证据强度
该研究因其对行政索赔数据的依赖而受到限制,这可能会引入偏见和信息缺失。然而,证据是强有力的,队列研究 的大小很大,并且对多个混杂因素进行了调整。

2024-2025 BNT162b2 KP.2 COVID-19 full season vaccine effectiveness from vaccine registries linked to administrative claims in two states: A cohort study in non-immunocompromised adults.

Authors: Tara Ahi, Andersen KM, Mateus JS, Tiange Yu, Anan Zhou, Nepal RM, Santiago M. C. Lopez, L. Puzniak
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND Data on effectiveness of COVID-19 vaccinations during the 2024-2025 respiratory season are limited, particularly among those with underlying medical conditions (UMC). We estimated BNT162b2 KP.2 vaccine effectiveness (VE) against COVID-19-associated hospital admission, emergency department (ED), and urgent care (UC) visits in two U.S. METHODS Retrospective cohort study of non-immunocompromised adults living in Louisiana or California, with ≥1 year prior continuous enrollment in insurance plans contributing to the HealthVerity claims database beginning August 22, 2024. The effectiveness of BNT162b2 KP.2 vaccine (2024-2025 formulation, hereafter referred to as BNT162b2), measured as a time-varying exposure against hospital admission, ED, or UC encounters with International Classification of Diseases, Tenth Revision, Clinical Modification (ICD-10-CM) code U07.1 was calculated as 1 - adjusted hazard ratio using Cox proportional hazard models adjusted for age group, sex, state, insurance payor, presence or absence of UMCs, and pre-index healthcare utilization. Stratifications included those aged 65 years and older, those aged 18-64 years with UMCs, and those aged 18-64 years without UMCs. RESULTS The cohort included 6,256,421 individuals (93% California, 7% Louisiana); 330,565 (5%) received the BNT162b2 vaccine. Vaccinated individuals were older and had more comorbidities, wellness visits, and prior influenza vaccination. Overall, 66% of the study population had ≥1 UMC; the most prevalent conditions were obesity (25%), history of immunocompromised conditions (23%), and mental health conditions (19%). COVID-19-related encounter rates for ED, UC or hospitalization were lower among vaccinated compared to unvaccinated persons (25.1 vs 36.3 per 100,000 person-months). Among all adults, VE was 37% against hospitalization, 12% against ED/UC encounters, and 16% against ED/UC/hospitalization encounters. Results were similar across age groups and UMCs. CONCLUSIONS BNT162b2 provided protection against COVID-19-associated outcomes of ED, UC or hospitalization among non-immunocompromised U.S. adults, including those with UMCs, over the course of the 2024-2025 respiratory virus season, supporting continued vaccine recommendations. REGISTRATION This study was posted on clinicaltrials.gov prior to analyses (NCT06923137).
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to assess the effectiveness of the BNT162b2 KP.2 vaccine against COVID-19-associated hospital admissions, emergency department visits, and urgent care encounters during the 2024-2025 respiratory season, as data on this topic are limited, especially among individuals with underlying medical conditions.
Design and population
A retrospective cohort study was conducted among non-immunocompromised adults in Louisiana and California, with ≥1 year of prior continuous insurance enrollment in the HealthVerity claims database starting August 22, 2024. The cohort included 6,256,421 individuals, of whom 330,565 (5%) received the BNT162b2 vaccine.
Core methods
The study used a time-varying exposure approach to calculate vaccine effectiveness against hospital admission, emergency department, or urgent care encounters using ICD-10-CM code U07.1. Adjusted hazard ratios were estimated via Cox proportional hazard models, accounting for age group, sex, state, insurance payor, presence or absence of underlying medical conditions, and prior healthcare utilization.
Main results
The effectiveness of BNT162b2 against hospitalization was 37%, against emergency department/urgent care encounters was 12%, and against all three outcomes combined was 16%. These results were consistent across age groups and underlying medical conditions.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors concluded that the BNT162b2 vaccine provided protection against COVID-19-associated outcomes, including hospitalization, emergency department, and urgent care visits, among non-immunocompromised U.S. adults during the 2024-2025 respiratory virus season.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings support continued vaccine recommendations for non-immunocompromised adults, including those with underlying medical conditions, during the 2024-2025 respiratory season, highlighting the vaccine's role in reducing severe outcomes of COVID-19.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was limited by its reliance on administrative claims data, which may introduce biases and missing information. However, the evidence is strong, with a large cohort size and adjustment for multiple confounding factors.

埃塞俄比亚亚的斯亚贝巴市医务人员对严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2感染的预防与控制措施依从性:暴发的未来防控启示

Infection prevention and control adherence among SARS-CoV-2 infected healthcare workers in Addis Ababa, Ethiopia: lessons for future outbreaks.
作者： Zekarias Amdemariam, Tewodros Eshete, Ruth Woldeyohannes Yirgu, Birhane Redae, Woldesenbet Wagnew, Shewalem Geremew, Amanuel Hintsa, Bezawit Endeshaw, Miraf Walelegn, Sisay Yifru
摘要中文翻译背景 从 暴发 到埃塞俄比亚 2025-2026 年马尔堡 暴发,医护人员 (HCW) 感染高后果病原体,凸显了感染预防和控制 (IPC) 方面持续存在的差距。我们评估了埃塞俄比亚亚的斯亚贝巴受感染医护人员的 IPC 依从性及其决定因素,为未来的 暴发 准备工作提供信息。方法 这项基于人口普查的横断面研究(2020 年 3 月至 2021 年 3 月)评估了 1152 名感染 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 的医护人员,他们在 诊断 发生前 14 天有临床暴露。通过改编的 OSHA 金字塔将环境分为:高病原体指定区域隔离和 治疗 单位、一般高风险区域 [重症监护室、急诊科和手术室]、中风险临床区域 [住院和门诊部门] 和低风险支持区域 [行政和非临床区域]。遵守被定义为在超过 50% 的互动中采取正确的做法。双变量卡方检验和多变量逻辑回归确定了穿/脱 (n = 742) 和 N95 密封检查 (n = 350) 的独立决定因素,报告调整后的比值比 (aOR),P 结果置信区间为 95% 只有 19.2% 的感染医护人员在高病原体指定区域工作。口罩 (85.7-97.7%) 和手套 (68.2-98.0%) 的依从性很高,但长袍、工作服、头帽和鞋套的依从性低于 50%。例外情况包括面罩/护目镜 (52.8%) 和在气溶胶产生程序 (AGP) 期间使用 N95 (66.5%)。N95 密封检查合规率为 34.9%;只有 1.1% 使用经过适合性测试的呼吸器。手部卫生的比例从接触前的 49.3% 上升到接触液体后的 82.5%。卡方分析将医疗保健专业人员和高病原体指定区域状态与显着优越的依从性联系起来。对于穿/脱,非高病原体指定区域放置(一般高风险区域 aOR = 0.12 (0.07-0.19);中风险临床区域 aOR = 0.08 (0.04-0.14);低风险支持区域 [aOR = 0.07 (0.03-0.17)] 和支持人员 [aOR = 0.35 (0.14-0.88)];AGP 参与 [aOR = 3.61 (2.33-5.58)] 和 IPC 训练 [aOR = 1.59 (1.05-2.41)] 是一般高风险区域中的阳性预测因子 aOR = 0.53 (0.30-0.93);aOR = 0.33 (0.16-0.66);低风险支持区域 [aOR = 0.25 (0.08-0.77)]。阳性预测因子包括公共生活 [aOR = 2.37 (1.29-4.35)]、IPC 培训 [aOR = 1.71 (1.03-2.83)] 和 AGP 参与 [aOR = 1.71 (1.05-2.80)],而年龄 > 30 [aOR = 0.57 (0.34-0.98)] 和压力 [aOR = 0.46(0.28-0.77)]是负决定因素。结论 这项研究强调了医护人员之间的 IPC 差距,特别是在大多数感染发生的非指定区域,强调了所有医疗环境中标准化 IPC 的必要性。

问题与背景
医务人员(HCW)在感染高后果病原体期间的感染情况,从[严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2]至埃塞俄比亚2025-2026年马尔堡病暴发,凸显了预防与控制(IPC)措施长期存在的缺口。

Infection prevention and control adherence among SARS-CoV-2 infected healthcare workers in Addis Ababa, Ethiopia: lessons for future outbreaks.

Authors: Zekarias Amdemariam, Tewodros Eshete, Ruth Woldeyohannes Yirgu, Birhane Redae, Woldesenbet Wagnew, Shewalem Geremew, Amanuel Hintsa, Bezawit Endeshaw, Miraf Walelegn, Sisay Yifru
Original abstract (English)
Background Health care worker (HCW) infections during outbreaks of high-consequence pathogens, from SARS-CoV-2 to Ethiopia's 2025-2026 Marburg outbreak, highlight persistent Infection Prevention and Control (IPC) gaps. We evaluated IPC adherence and its determinants among infected HCWs in Addis Ababa, Ethiopia, to inform future outbreak preparedness. Methods This census-based cross-sectional study (March 2020-March 2021) evaluated 1152 SARS-CoV-2-infected HCWs with clinical exposures 14 days pre-diagnosis. Environments were classified via an adapted OSHA pyramid into: High-Pathogen Designated Areas isolation and treatment units, General High-Risk Areas [intensive care units, emergency departments, and operating theaters], Medium-Risk Clinical Areas [inpatient and outpatient departments], and Low-Risk Support Areas [administrative and non-clinical zones]. Adherence was defined as correct practice in > 50% of interactions. Bivariate chi-square tests and multivariable logistic regression identified independent determinants for donning/doffing (n = 742) and N95 seal-checks (n = 350), reporting adjusted odds ratios (aOR) with 95% confidence intervals at P Results Only 19.2% of infected HCWs worked in High-Pathogen Designated Areas. Mask (85.7-97.7%) and glove (68.2-98.0%) adherence was high, but fell below 50% for gowns, coveralls, head caps, and shoe covers. Exceptions included face shields/goggles (52.8%) and N95 use during aerosol-generating procedures (AGPs) (66.5%). N95 seal-check compliance was 34.9%; only 1.1% used fit-tested respirators. Hand hygiene rose from 49.3% pre-interaction to 82.5% post-fluid exposure. Chi-square analyses linked health care professionasl and High-Pathogen Designated Area status with significantly superior adherence. For donning/doffing, lower compliance was predicted by non-High-Pathogen Designated Area placement (General High-Risk Areas aOR = 0.12 (0.07-0.19); Medium-Risk Clinical Areas aOR = 0.08 (0.04-0.14); Low-Risk Support Areas [aOR = 0.07 (0.03-0.17)] and support staff [aOR = 0.35 (0.14-0.88)]; AGP involvement [aOR = 3.61 (2.33-5.58)] and IPC training [aOR = 1.59 (1.05-2.41)] were positive predictors. N95 seal-check odds were lower in General High-Risk Areas aOR = 0.53 (0.30-0.93); Medium-Risk Clinical Areas aOR = 0.33 (0.16-0.66); Low-Risk Support Areas [aOR = 0.25 (0.08-0.77)]. Positive predictors included communal living [aOR = 2.37 (1.29-4.35)], IPC training [aOR = 1.71 (1.03-2.83)], and AGP involvement [aOR = 1.71 (1.05-2.80)], while age > 30 [aOR = 0.57 (0.34-0.98)] and stress [aOR = 0.46 (0.28-0.77)] were negative determinants. Conclusions This study underscores IPC gaps among HCWs especially in non-designated areas, where most infections occurred, highlighting the need for standardized IPC across all healthcare environments.

Scientific and public-health significance
